  177:     /** FIX-11H explicit replay-only OLD/NEW selector. Production is not configurable through this mode. */
  178:     public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
  179:         if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
  180:                 || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
  181:                 || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
  182:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol, start time and end time are required, and end must be after start.");
  183:         }
  184: 
  185:         Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
  186:                 SELECT COUNT(*)
  187:                 FROM analysis_test_run
  188:                 WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
  189:                 """, Integer.class);
  190:         if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
  191:             Map<String, Object> active = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap("""
  192:                     SELECT id, test_name, symbol, status, progress_percent, started_at, created_at
  193:                     FROM analysis_test_run
  194:                     WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
  195:                     ORDER BY id DESC
  196:                     LIMIT 1
  197:                     """);
  198:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
  199:                     "Regression test #" + active.get("id") + " is already " + active.get("status")
  200:                             + " for " + active.get("symbol") + ". Wait for it to finish before starting another run.");
  201:         }
  202: 
  203:         String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  204:         ReplayDataSource effectiveSource = dataSource == null ? ReplayDataSource.DATABASE : dataSource;
  205:         String baseTestName = request.testName() == null || request.testName().isBlank()
  206:                 ? symbol + " regression" : request.testName().trim();
  207:         String testName = (baseTestName + " [" + effectiveSource.name() + "]").substring(
  208:                 0, Math.min(180, baseTestName.length() + effectiveSource.name().length() + 3));

    1: package com.crypto.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.config.AnalysisScoringProperties;
    4: import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
    5: import com.crypto.dto.CandleDataQualityResult;
    6: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
    7: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
    8: import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
    9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   10: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
   11: 
   12: import java.time.Duration;
   13: import java.util.ArrayList;
   14: import java.util.Comparator;
   15: import java.util.List;
   16: 
   17: @Service
   18: @RequiredArgsConstructor
   19: public class CandleDataQualityService {
   20: 
   21:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
   22:     private final AnalysisScoringProperties properties;
   23: 
   24:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
   25:     public CandleDataQualityResult validate(String symbol, String intervalCode) {
   26:         int minimum = properties.dataQuality().minimumCandles();
   27:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
   28:                 symbol, intervalCode, PageRequest.of(0, minimum)
   29:         ));
   30:         candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));
   31: 
   32:         List<String> warnings = new ArrayList<>();
   33:         int invalid = 0;
   34:         for (Candle candle : candles) {
   35:             boolean invalidOhlc = candle.getOpenPrice() == null
   36:                     || candle.getHighPrice() == null
   37:                     || candle.getLowPrice() == null
   38:                     || candle.getClosePrice() == null
   39:                     || candle.getVolume() == null
   40:                     || candle.getOpenPrice().signum() <= 0
   41:                     || candle.getHighPrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
   42:                     || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
   43:                     || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getHighPrice()) > 0
   44:                     || candle.getVolume().signum() < 0;
   45:             if (invalidOhlc) {
   46:                 invalid++;
   47:             }
   48:         }
   49: 
   50:         Duration expectedStep = intervalDuration(intervalCode);
   51:         int missing = 0;
   52:         if (!expectedStep.isZero()) {
   53:             for (int index = 1; index < candles.size(); index++) {
   54:                 Duration actual = Duration.between(candles.get(index - 1).getOpenTime(), candles.get(index).getOpenTime());
   55:                 if (actual.compareTo(expectedStep) > 0) {
   56:                     long steps = actual.toMillis() / expectedStep.toMillis();
   57:                     missing += Math.max(0, (int) steps - 1);
   58:                 }
   59:             }
   60:         } else {
   61:             warnings.add("Unsupported interval for gap validation: " + intervalCode);
   62:         }
   63: 
   64:         if (candles.size() < minimum) {
   65:             warnings.add("Insufficient history: " + candles.size() + "/" + minimum + " closed candles");
   66:         }
   67:         if (missing > 0) {
   68:             warnings.add("Missing " + missing + " candle(s) in the analysis window");
   69:         }
   70:         if (invalid > 0) {
   71:             warnings.add("Found " + invalid + " candle(s) with invalid OHLCV data");
   72:         }
   73: 
   74:         boolean valid = candles.size() >= minimum
   75:                 && missing <= properties.dataQuality().maximumMissingCandles()
   76:                 && (!properties.dataQuality().blockOnInvalidOhlc() || invalid == 0);
   77: 
   78:         return new CandleDataQualityResult(valid, minimum, candles.size(), missing, invalid, List.copyOf(warnings));
   79:     }
   80: 
   81:     private Duration intervalDuration(String intervalCode) {
   82:         if (intervalCode == null || intervalCode.length() < 2) {
   83:             return Duration.ZERO;
   84:         }
   85:         try {
   86:             long amount = Long.parseLong(intervalCode.substring(0, intervalCode.length() - 1));
   87:             char unit = intervalCode.charAt(intervalCode.length() - 1);
   88:             return switch (unit) {
   89:                 case 'm' -> Duration.ofMinutes(amount);
   90:                 case 'h' -> Duration.ofHours(amount);
   91:                 case 'd' -> Duration.ofDays(amount);
   92:                 case 'w' -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 7);
   93:                 default -> Duration.ZERO;
   94:             };
   95:         } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
   96:             return Duration.ZERO;
   97:         }
   98:     }
   99: }

  130:      * It reads the latest already-persisted technical indicator.

  142: 
  143:     /**
  144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
  145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
  146:      */
  147:     @Transactional
  148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
  149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
  150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
  151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
  152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
  153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
  154:     }
  155: 
  156:     /**
  157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
  158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
  159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
  160:      *
  161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
  162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
  163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
  164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
  165:      */
  166:     @Transactional
  167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
  168:         if (indicator == null) {
  169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
  170:         }
  171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
  172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
  173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
  174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
  175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
  176:     }
  177:

  188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
  189:         try {
  190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);

  130:      * FIX-11H REPLAY/PARITY INFRASTRUCTURE ONLY. Production calculateAndPersist() never calls

  132:      * calculateSnapshot() below remains the exact shared indicator math.
  133:      */
  134:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  135:     public Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> calculateSnapshotForRegression(
  136:             String symbol, String intervalCode, Instant candleOpenTime, ReplayDataset dataset) {
  137:         if (candleOpenTime == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression candle open time is required");
  138:         String normalisedSymbol = normaliseSymbol(symbol);
  139:         String normalisedInterval = normaliseInterval(intervalCode);
  140:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(
  141:                 dataset.closedCandlesAtOrBefore(normalisedSymbol, normalisedInterval, candleOpenTime, HISTORY_LIMIT));
  142:         if (candles.size() < MINIMUM_CANDLES) return Optional.empty();
  143:         Collections.reverse(candles);
  144:         return Optional.of(calculateSnapshot(normalisedSymbol, normalisedInterval, candles));
  145:     }

  175:     calculateSnapshotFromDatabase(
  176:             String symbol,
  177:             String intervalCode,
  178:             Instant maxOpenTime
  179:     ) {
  180:         String normalisedSymbol =
  181:                 normaliseSymbol(symbol);
  182: 
  183:         String normalisedInterval =
  184:                 normaliseInterval(intervalCode);
  185: 
  186:         List<Candle> candles =
  187:                 new ArrayList<>(
  188:                         maxOpenTime == null
  189:                                 ? candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
  190:                                         normalisedSymbol,
  191:                                         normalisedInterval,
  192:                                         PageRequest.of(0, HISTORY_LIMIT)
  193:                                 )
  194:                                 : candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
  195:                                         normalisedSymbol,
  196:                                         normalisedInterval,
  197:                                         maxOpenTime,
  198:                                         PageRequest.of(0, HISTORY_LIMIT)
  199:                                 )
  200:                 );
  201: 
  202:         if (candles.size() < MINIMUM_CANDLES) {
  203:             log.warn(
  204:                     "Indicator calculation skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, closedCandles={}, required={}",
  205:                     normalisedSymbol,
  206:                     normalisedInterval,
  207:                     candles.size(),
  208:                     MINIMUM_CANDLES
  209:             );
  210:             return Optional.empty();
  211:         }
  212: 
  213:         Collections.reverse(candles);
  214: 
  215:         log.info(
  216:         	    "Calculating indicators before snapshot for symbol={}, interval={}",
  217:         	    symbol,
  218:         	    intervalCode
  219:         	);

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java 1-100 =====
    1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
    4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
    5: 
    6: import java.util.ArrayList;
    7: import java.util.List;
    8: import java.util.Objects;
    9: 
   10: /**
   11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
   12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
   13:  */
   14: @Service
   15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
   16: 
   17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
   18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
   19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
   20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
   21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
   22:         }
   23: 
   24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
   25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
   26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
   27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
   28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
   29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
   30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
   31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
   32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
   33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
   34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
   35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
   36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
   37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
   38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
   39:     }
   40: 
   41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
   42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
   43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
   44:         }
   45:     }
   46: 
   47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
   48: }

   61:     public ReplayStats replay(long runId, String symbol, Instant executionStart, Instant executionEnd,
   62:                               List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals,
   63:                               List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> productionPriceEvents,
   64:                               BooleanSupplier stopRequested) {
   65:         List<TradeSignal> timeline = generatedSignals.stream()
   66:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
   67:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt)
   68:                         .thenComparingInt(s -> intervalOrder(s.getInterval())))
   69:                 .toList();
   70: 
   71:         TradeSignal latest1m = null;
   72:         TradeSignal latest5m = null;
   73:         TradeSignal latest1h = null;
   74:         ShadowPosition open = null;
   75:         BigDecimal cash = INITIAL_CAPITAL;
   76:         BigDecimal realized = BigDecimal.ZERO;
   77:         int trades = 0, wins = 0, losses = 0;
   78:         int consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = 0;
   79:         WalletSettings replayWalletSettings = walletSettings();
   80:         BigDecimal replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
   81:                 cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
   82:                 replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
   83:         LocalDate replayTradeDate = null;
   84:         int replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
   85:         // FIX-052: when Production live-price events exist, Replay uses them as the
   86:         // authoritative mechanical-protection clock. Older windows created before V64
   87:         // transparently retain candle/signal-close fallback and are therefore not claimed
   88:         // as tick-exact parity runs.
   89:         List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> livePrices = productionPriceEvents == null
   90:                 ? List.of()
   91:                 : productionPriceEvents.stream()
   92:                     .filter(e -> e != null && e.observedAt() != null && e.price() != null)
   93:                     .sorted(Comparator.comparing(MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent::observedAt))
   94:                     .toList();
   95:         boolean exactPriceReplay = !livePrices.isEmpty();
   96:         int livePriceIndex = 0;
   97:         // FIX-109: normal Regression/Replay runs are Production-Parity by default.
   98:         // Experimental rules require an explicitly opened EXPERIMENTAL scope.
   99:         String replayLogicMode = ExecutionReplayScope.ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY.name();
  100:         defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunStart(runId, symbol);
  101:         oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.startRun(runId, symbol);
  102: 
  103:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, timeline,
  104:                 opportunity -> {
  105:                     TradeSignal latest = opportunity == null ? null : opportunity.getLatestSignal();
  106:                     if (latest != null && latest.getGeneratedAt() != null
  107:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
  108:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
  109:                         persistProductionOpportunity(runId, opportunity);
  110:                     }
  111:                 })) {
  112:         // FIX-122: independent OLD comparison revision; ordinary Replay defaults to NEW.
  113:         java.util.List<Boolean> revisions = jdbcTemplate.query(
  114:                 "SELECT enabled FROM fix122_replay_revision WHERE test_run_id=?",
  115:                 (rs,row) -> rs.getBoolean(1), runId);
  116:         replayScope.fix122Enabled(revisions == null || revisions.isEmpty() || revisions.get(0));
  117:         for (TradeSignal signal : timeline) {
  118:             // FIX-090: shadow execution is frequently cancellable without affecting Production.
  119:             if (stopRequested != null && stopRequested.getAsBoolean()) {
  120:                 throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
  121:             }
  122:             // FIX-052 exact ordering: BinanceKlineService invokes live position protection
  123:             // from the 1m price update BEFORE publishing the candle-close analysis event.
  124:             // Consume all persisted Production prices up to this signal timestamp before
  125:             // making the freshly generated signal visible to position management.
  126:             while (livePriceIndex < livePrices.size()
  127:                     && (stopRequested == null || !stopRequested.getAsBoolean())
  128:                     && !livePrices.get(livePriceIndex).observedAt().isAfter(signal.getGeneratedAt())) {
  129:                 MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent live = livePrices.get(livePriceIndex++);
  130:                 replayScope.referencePrice(symbol, live.observedAt(), live.price());
  131:                 // FIX-11Q: advance only the isolated counterfactual grace position. This never mutates
  132:                 // the baseline Shadow position/cash/trades and therefore cannot change Replay parity.
  133:                 oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
  134:                 if (open != null && !live.observedAt().isBefore(executionStart) && !live.observedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
  135:                     LivePriceEvaluation protection = evaluateLivePrice(runId, symbol, open, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
  136:                     open = protection.position();
  137:                     if (protection.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
  138:                         cash = cash.add(protection.partialProceeds(), MC);
  139:                         realized = realized.add(protection.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
  140:                     }
  141:                     ExitDecision liveExit = protection.decision();
  142:                     if (liveExit.exit()) {
  143:                         BigDecimal proceeds = live.price().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
  144:                         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
  145:                         BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), live.price());
  146:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
  147:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
  148:                         cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
  149:                         realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
  150:                         trades++;
  151:                         if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
  152:                         // wallet_execution_test stores this terminal leg; wallet_position_test stores
  153:                         // the whole lifecycle including any earlier FIX-11T partial realization.
  154:                         persistSellAtPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, open, liveExit, pnl, pnlPct);
  155:                         closePositionAtPrice(runId, open.positionId(), live, liveExit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
  156:                         replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), live.observedAt(), liveExit.reason());
  157:                         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, latest1m, liveExit.reason());
  158:                                                 open = null;
  159:                     }
  160:                 }
  161:             }
  162: 
  163:             replayScope.reference(signal.getGeneratedAt());
  164:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
  165:                 latest1m = signal;
  166:                 consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
  167:                         ? consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells + 1 : 0;
  168:             }
  169:             if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest5m = signal;
  170:             if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest1h = signal;
  171:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onSignal(runId, signal, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
  172: 
  173:             // FIX-11K Phase A: observe only. This runs beside the existing exit path and cannot
  174:             // mutate quantity, cash, wallet_position_test, SELL authority, or Production state.
  175:             // It records enough replay-native evidence to evaluate the 2/3/4 streak and
  176:             // peak/giveback experiment matrix after the run instead of pre-selecting thresholds.
  177:             if (open != null && "1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
  178:                 defensiveRiskReductionObserver.observe(
  179:                         runId, open.positionId(), signal, latest5m, latest1h,
  180:                         consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells, open.entryPrice(), open.highest());
  181:             }
  182: 
  183:             if (open != null && !exactPriceReplay) {
  184:                 // Pre-V64 fallback only. Exact-parity runs receive mechanical protection from
  185:                 // the persisted Production live-price stream above.
  186:                 ExitDecision exit = evaluateExit(runId, open, signal, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
  187:                 if (exit.exit()) {
  188:                     BigDecimal proceeds = signal.getLatestPrice().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
  189:                     BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
  190:                     BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  191:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
  192:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
  193:                     cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
  194:                     realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
  195:                     trades++;
  196:                     if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
  197:                     persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
  198:                     closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
  199:                     // FIX-020 replay parity: a terminal replay exit consumes the same
  200:                     // opportunity evidence boundary as production before any new BUY can form.
  201:                     replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
  202:                     executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());
  203:                                         open = null;
  204:                     // Production PaperTradingService returns immediately after a signal-driven
  205:                     // terminal close; it cannot reopen from that same signal invocation.
  206:                     continue;
  207:                 } else {
  208:                     if (exit.newTakeProfit() != null) {
  209:                         open = rebaseForExtendedTarget(open, exit.newTakeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt())
  210:                                 .withTakeProfit(exit.newTakeProfit());
  211:                         persistProfitLockState(open);
  212:                         persistNearTpState(open);
  213:                     }
  214:                     open = updateProfitLock(open, signal);
  215:                     // FIX-11T: legacy signal-price fallback cannot be tick-exact, but it still
  216:                     // executes the SAME Near-TP policy from the authoritative historical signal
  217:                     // price so older Replay windows do not silently omit the Production rule.
  218:                     if (priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, open.entryTime())) {
  219:                         FallbackNearTpEvaluation nearTpFallback = evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(
  220:                                 runId, open, signal, latest1m, latest5m);
  221:                         open = nearTpFallback.position();
  222:                         if (nearTpFallback.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
  223:                             cash = cash.add(nearTpFallback.partialProceeds(), MC);
  224:                             realized = realized.add(nearTpFallback.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
  225:                         }
  226:                     }
  227:                     persistManagement(runId, signal, open.profitLockActive() ? "PROFIT_LOCK_ACTIVE" : "POSITION_HOLD",
  228:                             open.takeProfit(), open.takeProfit(), open, exit.explanation());
  229:                 }
  230:             }
  231: 
  232:             if (open != null && exactPriceReplay && "1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && bearish(signal.getDecision())) {
  233:                 // Production's candle-close PaperTradingService has one additional SELL authority
  234:                 // after live-price protection: validate the newly generated 1m bearish signal.
  235:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
  236:                 if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
  237:                     ExitDecision exit = new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
  238:                     BigDecimal proceeds = signal.getLatestPrice().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
  239:                     BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
  240:                     BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  241:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
  242:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
  243:                     cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
  244:                     realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
  245:                     trades++;
  246:                     if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
  247:                     persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
  248:                     closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
  249:                     replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
  250:                     executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());
  251:                                         open = null;
  252:                     // Same production invocation cannot both SELL and immediately BUY again.
  253:                     continue;
  254:                 }
  255:             }
  256: 
  257:             // FIX-014 replay parity: production may use a fresh 5m BUY transition only to
  258:             // wake an already-live deferred 1m opportunity. The actual execution/risk plan
  259:             // remains the latest fresh 1m signal returned by the shared production service.
  260:             // All other non-1m signals remain context-only exactly as before.
  261:             TradeSignal executionSignal = signal;
  262:             ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision decision;
  263:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
  264:                 int currentAllocation = open == null ? 0 : open.positionPercent();
  265:                 String currentStage = replayStage(currentAllocation);
  266:                 // FIX-026 parity: Recovery/Transition Entry is intentionally NOT reimplemented here.
  267:                 // Replay enters the exact production ExecutionIntelligenceService, which delegates to
  268:                 // RecoveryTransitionService using only candles closed as-of this historical signal.
  269:                 // This protects the ENA 12:55-13:04 KSA regression from replay-only future leakage.
  270:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(signal, currentAllocation, currentStage);
  271:             } else if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && open == null) {
  272:                 ExecutionIntelligenceService.SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup =
  273:                         executionIntelligenceService.evaluateSetupTimeframeWakeup(signal, 0);
  274:                 if (!wakeup.present()) {
  275:                     // FIX-023 replay parity: use the exact same production confirmation wake-up
  276:                     // after liquidity/HTF improves; 5m remains context-only execution authority.
  277:                     wakeup = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeup(signal, 0);
  278:                 }
  279:                 if (!wakeup.present()) continue;
  280:                 executionSignal = wakeup.executionSignal();
  281:                 decision = wakeup.decision();
  282:             } else {
  283:                 continue;
  284:             }
  285: 
  286:             // Warm-up evaluates the SAME production decision service so evidence/opportunity
  287:             // memory is realistic at executionStart, but it never opens or adds to a wallet
  288:             // position before the requested test window. The trigger timestamp (signal) controls
  289:             // the replay window; executionSignal owns price/risk just like production.
  290:             boolean inExecutionWindow = !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
  291:                     && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd);
  292:             LocalDate signalTradeDate = signal.getGeneratedAt().atZone(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate();
  293:             if (!signalTradeDate.equals(replayTradeDate)) {
  294:                 replayTradeDate = signalTradeDate;
  295:                 replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
  296:                 replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
  297:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
  298:                         replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
  299:             }
  300:             // IMPORTANT: Proven/Regression never re-implements pressure readiness, normal BUY
  301:             // routing, or FIX-014 wake-up rules. All are executed by the production
  302:             // ExecutionIntelligenceService above; only wallet/opportunity persistence is shadowed.
  303:             if (!inExecutionWindow) continue;
  304: 
  305:             if (open == null && decision.allowed()) {
  306:                 // FIX-056 Replay parity: resolve the same canonical execution-price authority
  307:                 // after the same Production decision gates, then revalidate Entry Quality at
  308:                 // that price before sizing. A replay without a fresh price event cannot claim
  309:                 // exact execution parity and therefore does not open the position here.
  310:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
  311:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  312:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
  313:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(executionSignal, decision, fresh.price());
  314:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
  315:                 int atrPercent = executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() <= 0 ? 100 : executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent();
  316:                 int effectivePercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100,
  317:                         (int)Math.round(atrPercent * decision.positionPercent() / 100.0)));
  318:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
  319:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
  320:                         effectivePercent, 0, true, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
  321:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
  322:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
  323:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
  324:                     BigDecimal qty = sizing.quantity();
  325:                     effectivePercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
  326:                     long positionId = openPosition(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent);
  327:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent, decision);
  328:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, decision);
  329:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
  330:                     replayExecutedNewPositions++;
  331:                     // Keep the complete immutable BUY thesis needed by production continuation.
  332:                     // Replay must not drop entry structure and silently evaluate a different exit policy.
  333:                     open = new ShadowPosition(positionId, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), fresh.price(), qty, budget,
  334:                             effectivePercent, executionSignal.getStopLoss(), executionSignal.getTakeProfit(), fresh.price(), false, null,
  335:                             ProfitLockState.INACTIVE, null,
  336:                             executionSignal.getTotalScore(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), executionSignal.getTrendScore(), executionSignal.getTrendStructureScore(),
  337:                             executionSignal.getMomentumScore(), executionSignal.getVolumeScore(),
  338:                             NearTpState.INACTIVE, null, 0, null, false, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
  339:                     // FIX-112A: only an actually opened shadow position consumes the bullish entry.
  340:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(executionSignal.getId());
  341:                 }
  342:             } else if (open != null && decision.allowed()) {
  343:                 // FIX-056: progressive Replay adds use the same fresh execution price and
  344:                 // execution-time Entry Quality revalidation as Production.
  345:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
  346:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  347:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
  348:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, decision, fresh.price());
  349:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
  350:                 int addPercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100 - open.positionPercent(), decision.positionPercent()));
  351:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
  352:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
  353:                         addPercent, open.positionPercent(), false, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
  354:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
  355:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
  356:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
  357:                     addPercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
  358:                     BigDecimal addedQty = sizing.quantity();
  359:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, signal, fresh, addedQty, budget, addPercent, decision);
  360:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(signal, decision);
  361:                     // FIX-112A: a progressive add consumes only its own triggering signal.
  362:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(signal.getId());
  363:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
  364:                     open = addToPosition(runId, open, signal, addedQty, budget, addPercent);
  365:                 }
  366:             }
  367:         }
  368:         }
  369: 
  370:         BigDecimal finalWallet = cash;
  371:         if (open != null && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
  372:             BigDecimal mark = timeline.get(timeline.size() - 1).getLatestPrice();
  373:             if (mark != null) finalWallet = finalWallet.add(mark.multiply(open.quantity(), MC), MC);
  374:         }
  375: 
  376:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
  377:                 UPDATE analysis_test_run
  378:                 SET simulated_trade_count=?, simulated_win_count=?, simulated_loss_count=?,
  379:                     simulated_realized_pnl=?, simulated_final_wallet=?
  380:                 WHERE id=?
  381:                 """, trades, wins, losses, realized, finalWallet, runId);
  382:         defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunSummary(runId, symbol);
  383:         oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.finishRun(runId, symbol, executionEnd);
  384:         // FIX-109: expose whether this run had the persisted live-price stream required
  385:         // for tick-exact production ordering. Older windows remain explicitly labelled
  386:         // as fallback instead of being mistaken for exact parity.
  387:         return new ReplayStats(trades, wins, losses, realized, finalWallet,

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java 1-75 =====
    1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
    4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
    5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
    6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
    7: 
    8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
    9: import java.math.MathContext;
   10: import java.util.Locale;
   11: 
   12: /**
   13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
   14:  *
   15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
   16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
   17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
   18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
   19:  *
   20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
   21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
   22:  */
   23: @Component
   24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
   25: @Slf4j
   26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
   27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
   28: 
   29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
   30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
   31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
   32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
   33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
   34:         }
   35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
   36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
   37:         BigDecimal minimum;
   38:         try {
   39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
   40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
   41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
   42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
   43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
   44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
   45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
   46:         }
   47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
   48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
   49:         }
   50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
   51:     }
   52: 
   53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
   54:     }
   55: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java 1-240 =====
    1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
    4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
    5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
    6: 
    7: import java.time.Instant;
    8: import java.util.ArrayList;
    9: import java.util.Comparator;
   10: import java.util.List;
   11: import java.util.Optional;
   12: import java.util.HashSet;
   13: import java.util.Set;
   14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
   15: 
   16: /**
   17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
   18:  *
   19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
   20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
   21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
   22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
   23:  */
   24: @Component
   25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
   26: 
   27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
   28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
   29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
   30: 
   31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
   32: 
   33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
   34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
   35:     }
   36: 
   37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
   38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
   39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
   40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
   41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
   42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
   43:                 .toList();
   44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
   45:         return new Scope();
   46:     }
   47: 
   48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
   49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
   50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
   51:         State s=required();
   52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
   53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
   54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
   55:     }
   56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
   57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
   58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
   59:     }
   60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
   61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
   62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
   63: 
   64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
   65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
   66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
   67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
   68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
   69: 
   70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
   71: 
   72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
   73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
   74:         State s = required();
   75:         s.reference = observedAt;
   76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
   77:     }
   78: 
   79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
   80:         State s = required();
   81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
   82:     }
   83: 
   84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
   85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
   86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
   87:     }
   88: 
   89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
   90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
   91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
   92:     }
   93: 
   94:     /**
   95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
   96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
   97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
   98:      */
   99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
  100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
  101:         State s = required();
  102:         s.signals.add(signal);
  103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
  104:     }
  105: 
  106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
  107:         State s = required();
  108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
  109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
  110:         return s.signals.stream()
  111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
  112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
  113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
  114:                 .limit(limit)
  115:                 .toList();
  116:     }
  117: 
  118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
  119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
  120:     }
  121: 
  122:     /**
  123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
  124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
  125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
  126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
  127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
  128:      */
  129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
  130:         State s = required();
  131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
  132:         return s.signals.stream()
  133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
  134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
  135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
  136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
  137:     }
  138: 
  139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
  140:         State s = required();
  141:         return s.signals.stream()
  142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
  143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
  144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
  145:     }
  146: 
  147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
  148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
  149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
  150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
  151:         return Optional.of(o);
  152:     }
  153: 
  154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
  155:         State s = required();
  156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
  157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
  158:         return opportunity;
  159:     }
  160: 
  161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
  162:         private boolean closed;
  163:         @Override public void close() {
  164:             if (!closed) {
  165:                 state.remove();
  166:                 closed = true;
  167:             }
  168:         }
  169:     }
  170: 
  171:     private State required() {
  172:         State s = state.get();
  173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
  174:         return s;
  175:     }
  176: 
  177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
  178: 
  179:     private static final class State {
  180:         final long runId;
  181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
  182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
  183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
  184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
  185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
  186:         Instant reference;
  187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
  188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
  189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
  190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  191:             this.runId = runId;
  192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
  193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
  194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
  195:         }
  196:     }

  134:                 if (open != null && !live.observedAt().isBefore(executionStart) && !live.observedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
  135:                     LivePriceEvaluation protection = evaluateLivePrice(runId, symbol, open, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
  136:                     open = protection.position();
  137:                     if (protection.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
  138:                         cash = cash.add(protection.partialProceeds(), MC);
  139:                         realized = realized.add(protection.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
  140:                     }
  141:                     ExitDecision liveExit = protection.decision();
  142:                     if (liveExit.exit()) {
  143:                         BigDecimal proceeds = live.price().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
  144:                         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
  145:                         BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), live.price());
  146:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
  147:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
  148:                         cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
  149:                         realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
  150:                         trades++;
  151:                         if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
  152:                         // wallet_execution_test stores this terminal leg; wallet_position_test stores
  153:                         // the whole lifecycle including any earlier FIX-11T partial realization.
  154:                         persistSellAtPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, open, liveExit, pnl, pnlPct);
  155:                         closePositionAtPrice(runId, open.positionId(), live, liveExit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
  156:                         replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), live.observedAt(), liveExit.reason());
  157:                         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, latest1m, liveExit.reason());
  158:                                                 open = null;
  159:                     }
  160:                 }
  161:             }

  164:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {

 1018:     private BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPercent(ShadowPosition position, BigDecimal remainingLegPnl) {
 1019:         BigDecimal totalPnl = position.partialRealizedPnl().add(remainingLegPnl, MC);
 1020:         BigDecimal totalInvestedCost = position.cost().add(position.partialHarvestCostBasis(), MC);
 1021:         return totalInvestedCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 1022:                 : totalPnl.divide(totalInvestedCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 1023:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/config/SharedAnalysisExecutorConfig.java 1-60 =====
    1: package com.crypto.config;
    2: 
    3: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
    4: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
    5: import org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.ThreadPoolTaskExecutor;
    6: import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;
    7: 
    8: /** FIX-138: reserve live capacity; historical catch-up cannot consume it.
    9:  * No caller-runs fallback: a saturated pool must never execute on a dispatch clock. */
   10: @Configuration
   11: public class SharedAnalysisExecutorConfig {
   12:     // FIX-142: dispatcher and executor receive the same validated historical setting.
   13:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.historical-threads:1}")
   14:     private int historicalThreads=1;
   15:     @Bean("sharedLiveAnalysisExecutor")
   16:     public ThreadPoolTaskExecutor live() { return pool(8, "shared-live-"); }
   17: 
   18:     @Bean("sharedHistoricalAnalysisExecutor")
   19:     public ThreadPoolTaskExecutor historical() { if(historicalThreads<1||historicalThreads>4)throw new IllegalArgumentException("Historical capacity must be 1..4");return pool(historicalThreads, "shared-history-"); }
   20: 
   21:     private ThreadPoolTaskExecutor pool(int size, String prefix) {
   22:         var executor = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();
   23:         executor.setCorePoolSize(size);
   24:         executor.setMaxPoolSize(size);
   25:         // At most one admitted task per thread; the small queue only accommodates
   26:         // the interval between a task releasing its lane and returning to the pool.
   27:         executor.setQueueCapacity(size);
   28:         executor.setThreadNamePrefix(prefix);
   29:         executor.setRejectedExecutionHandler(new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
   30:         executor.setWaitForTasksToCompleteOnShutdown(true);
   31:         executor.setAwaitTerminationSeconds(30);
   32:         return executor;
   33:     }
   34: }

===== AGENTS.md 300-390 =====
  300: 
  301: The Coordinator must include this requirement in delegated prompts.
  302: 
  303: 
  304: ## Do Not Start From Zero
  305: 
  306: Existing agreed knowledge should be used as established project context.
  307: 
  308: Agents SHOULD NOT repeatedly rediscover established architecture without
  309: reason.
  310: 
  311: However, persistent documentation is not unquestionable truth.
  312: 
  313: If current code, database evidence, logs, tests, or runtime behavior
  314: contradict persistent knowledge:
  315: 
  316: 1. Report the contradiction.
  317: 2. Do NOT silently accept the old documentation.
  318: 3. Investigate the discrepancy.
  319: 4. Have both agents review the new evidence.
  320: 5. Update shared knowledge only through the dual-agent agreement process.
  321: 
  322: # MANDATORY PRE-IMPLEMENTATION CHANGE CONTRACT
  323: 
  324: This section extends the existing governance. It does not replace any earlier
  325: project, investigation, architecture, trading, safety, or documentation rule.
  326: 
  327: For any material code change, implementation is forbidden until the team has
  328: produced and approved the same exact revision of:
  329: 
  330: `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`
  331: 
  332: The specification is the implementation contract and MUST contain, at minimum:
  333: 
  334: 1. Business objective and the exact agreed business line/flow.
  335: 2. Confirmed root cause and evidence.
  336: 3. In-scope and out-of-scope boundaries.
  337: 4. Complete class/file change registry (modify/create/delete/rename/config/
  338:    migration/test/documentation).
  339: 5. Class-by-class and material method-by-method current behavior -> agreed new
  340:    behavior, reason, inputs/outputs, side effects, error handling, concurrency,
  341:    transactionality, Replay impact and Production impact.
  342: 6. Mandatory logging registry covering all materially affected areas. Logs must
  343:    make the business flow and decisions reconstructable without exposing
  344:    credentials/secrets. Include relevant correlation/trade/symbol/interval/
  345:    candle/test identifiers and respect the project's UTC/KSA rules.
  346: 7. Database, configuration and trading-behavior registries, explicitly stating
  347:    NONE when there is no impact.
  348: 8. Test and validation registry, including positive, negative, regression,
  349:    failure/recovery, concurrency where relevant, Replay, and Production-safe
  350:    validation.
  351: 9. Risks, rollback plan, observability plan and documentation registry.
  352: 10. Master implementation registry assigning an ID to every authorized change,
  353:     log, DB/config item, test and documentation update.
  354: 
  355: No source/config/database/trading change may be implemented unless it is
  356: registered in the approved specification. No unrelated refactoring or cleanup
  357: may be hidden inside an approved fix.
  358: 
  359: ## Dual-agent specification approval
  360: 
  361: The SAME exact specification revision must be fully reviewed and explicitly
  362: approved by BOTH agents:
  363: 
  364: - Codex: APPROVED
  365: - Claude: APPROVED
  366: - Unresolved material disagreements: 0
  367: 
  368: There is no majority vote and no fixed review-iteration limit. Any material
  369: revision invalidates both prior approvals and requires a complete re-review by
  370: both agents. Even after dual approval, implementation remains NOT AUTHORIZED
  371: until the user explicitly approves that exact revision.
  372: 
  373: ## Code implementation direction
  374: 
  375: After explicit user approval:
  376: 
  377: - Codex is the implementation owner.
  378: - Claude is the mandatory independent code reviewer.
  379: - Claude reviews the COMPLETE implementation, not only the latest patch.
  380: - Claude comments/rejects; Codex corrects or disputes with technical evidence;
  381:   Claude re-reviews the complete updated implementation.
  382: - Repeat for as many iterations as required. There is no fixed iteration cap.
  383: - Codex cannot self-approve its implementation and the Coordinator cannot
  384:   overrule a material Claude objection.
  385: - Any implementation-code change after Claude approval invalidates that
  386:   approval and requires another complete Claude review.
  387: - A material deviation from the approved specification stops implementation,
  388:   reopens dual-agent design review, creates a new specification revision, and
  389:   requires renewed user approval.
  390: 

===== agent-docs/PDF_ARTIFACT_RULES.md 1-40 =====
    1: # Frozen PDF Artifact Rules
    2: 
    3: Markdown remains the machine-readable source. A PDF is the frozen human-review artifact for major approval gates.
    4: 
    5: Mandatory frozen artifacts when the corresponding stage exists:
    6: - `FINAL_TECHNICAL_REVIEW-Rxx.pdf`
    7: - `FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION-Rxx.pdf`
    8: - `IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT-Rxx.pdf`
    9: - `VALIDATION_REPORT-Rxx.pdf`
   10: 
   11: Each frozen PDF must include the source revision, SHA-256 of the exact source Markdown, Codex verdict, Claude verdict, implementation authorization state, and a statement that any material source change invalidates the frozen revision.
   12: 
   13: Use `scripts/freeze-agent-artifact.ps1`. A dual-approved specification is not READY FOR USER REVIEW until its PDF freeze succeeds. PDF generation never grants implementation authorization.

===== src/main/resources/db/migration/V69__create_trade_signal_test_parity.sql 1-35 =====
    1: -- FIX-069: Replay keeps a full production-shaped TradeSignal snapshot for each generated signal.
    2: -- The table intentionally clones production trade_signal first, then adds only replay metadata.
    3: CREATE TABLE trade_signal_test LIKE trade_signal;
    4: -- Production enforces one signal per symbol/interval/candle. Replay must allow the same
    5: -- candle to be regenerated in multiple independent test runs, so run-scoping replaces
    6: -- the production-only uniqueness boundary.
    7: ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test DROP INDEX uk_trade_signal_symbol_interval_candle;
    8: ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test
    9:     ADD COLUMN test_run_id BIGINT NOT NULL AFTER id,
   10:     ADD COLUMN source_signal_id BIGINT NULL AFTER test_run_id,
   11:     ADD COLUMN replay_generated TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 AFTER source_signal_id,
   12:     ADD COLUMN generation_error VARCHAR(1000) NULL AFTER replay_generated,
   13:     ADD CONSTRAINT fk_trade_signal_test_run FOREIGN KEY (test_run_id) REFERENCES analysis_test_run(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   14:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_run_time (test_run_id, generated_at),
   15:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_run_interval (test_run_id, interval_code);
   16: 
   17: -- Safety archive remains backend-only even though the archive UI is removed.
   18: -- Composite key permits test-table IDs to restart from 1 after Clear Data.
   19: CREATE TABLE trade_signal_test_archive LIKE trade_signal_test;
   20: ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test_archive
   21:     DROP PRIMARY KEY,
   22:     MODIFY COLUMN id BIGINT NOT NULL,
   23:     ADD COLUMN archive_batch_id BIGINT NOT NULL FIRST,
   24:     ADD PRIMARY KEY (archive_batch_id, id),
   25:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_archive_run_time (archive_batch_id, test_run_id, generated_at);

   25:  * Frozen responsibilities:

   62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
   63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
   64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
   65: 
   66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();

   68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
   69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
   70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
   71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
   72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
   73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
   74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
   75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
   76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
   77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
   78:             }
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
   82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
   83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
   84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
   85:         }
   86:

   89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",

   95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java 115-285 =====
  115:     /**
  116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
  117:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
  118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
  119:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
  120:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
  121:      * normal BUY path would accept.
  122:      */
  123:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
  124:         return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
  125:     }
  126: 
  127:     /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
  128:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
  129:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  130:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
  131:         }
  132: 
  133:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  134:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  135:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  136:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
  137:         }
  138: 
  139:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  140:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  141:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  142:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
  143:         }
  144: 
  145:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  146:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  147:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  148:             audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
  149:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
  150:         }
  151: 
  152:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
  153:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
  154:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
  155:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
  156:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  157:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  158:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  159:         };
  160:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
  161:         audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
  162:         return result;
  163:     }

  165:     public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
  166:         ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
  167:         if (!base.allowed()) return base;
  168: 
  169:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  170:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  171:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  172:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
  173:         }
  174:         if (!isBearish(fiveMinute.getDecision())) {
  175:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BEARISH",
  176:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 5m decision was " + fiveMinute.getDecision() + ".");
  177:         }
  178: 
  179:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  180:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  181:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  182:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
  183:         }
  184:         if (isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())) {
  185:             return ValidationResult.reject("1H_BULLISH_VETO",
  186:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 1h strategic decision was " + oneHour.getDecision() + ".");
  187:         }
  188: 
  189:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "SELL_CONFIRMED",
  190:                 "1m SELL confirmed by fresh bearish 5m context; 1h is not bullish.");
  191:     }
